# Daily video prompt pack — 21/10/2026 · Dragon day (ថ្ងៃឆ្នាំរោង)

Lucky signs: Dragon, Rat, Monkey · Careful: Dog
Format: vertical 9:16 (YouTube Shorts, Facebook Reels, TikTok), about 45 seconds: 4 Veo scenes of about 8 seconds, then a lucky-numbers scene of about 10–12 seconds.
Two versions: make the pictures once, then record a Khmer voice-over and an English voice-over (two videos, or one video with both subtitle tracks).
Ratings, lucky signs and lucky numbers come from the site's fixed rules and match the website's daily fortune for this date.

## Luckiest animal today – Dragon (រោង) · Great Day
One hero post for the day's luckiest animal: a picture, a short video and a caption. Lucky color gold (មាស) · best time 15:00–17:00 · lucky numbers 1 · 65 · 110 · 6101 · 61747 (for fun only).

### Picture prompt (square 1:1)
Create a premium-quality 3D animated fantasy character of an adorable but mighty golden dragon with big friendly eyes, shimmering scales, flowing whiskers and a glowing pearl, the Dragon of the Chinese zodiac, inspired by the charm and enchanting visual appeal of classic Disney and Pixar-style animated family films. The dragon symbolizes power, good fortune and a noble spirit: a majestic, proud and noble expression and pose that show this. Body in creamy white-gold with shining engraved gold patterns, pink inner ears or rosy cheeks, polished gold textures, luminous highlights and delicate pink and gold accents. It sits on a round golden pedestal inside a large glowing pink lotus floating on a still pond. Behind it, a huge glowing full moon with a soft silver-gold halo, a deep indigo starry sky, a softly illuminated Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat with warm golden lights, palm tree silhouettes, small floating lanterns, glowing lotus flowers on the water, gentle moonbeams falling on the dragon, magical sparkles and soft silver-and-gold reflections. Cinematic lighting, soft atmospheric glow, realistic metallic reflections, beautiful depth of field, professional animated-movie-quality rendering. Square 1:1, full body visible, three-quarter view, centered, with space around the character. No text, no letters, no watermark, no logo, no cropped body parts.

## 60-second video – Facebook & TikTok
A 60-second video about the Dragon, today's luckiest animal: 6 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add the reference picture of the Dragon as an ingredient. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice": it copies the style line, the scene and the voice together. Put the clips in order 1 to 6.

### Topic and audience
Today's luckiest zodiac animal, the Dragon, in one minute. For viewers who follow Chinese zodiac signs, especially people born in the Year of the Dragon, scrolling Facebook Reels and TikTok.

### Opening hook – 5 options
1. Identity
   English: Born in the Year of the Dragon? Today is a Great Day for you. Here's your lucky color, time, and numbers.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរោងមែនទេ? ថ្ងៃនេះ ជាថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំងសម្រាប់អ្នក។ មកមើលពណ៌ ពេលវេលា និងលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នក។
★ 2. Curiosity
   English: Out of all twelve zodiac signs, only one has a Great Day today. Is it yours?
   Khmer: ក្នុងចំណោមរាសីទាំង 12 មានតែមួយគត់ ដែលមានថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំងនៅថ្ងៃនេះ។ តើជារាសីរបស់អ្នកឬទេ?
3. Question
   English: Which zodiac animal is the luckiest today? Here's the answer, and what it means for you.
   Khmer: តើឆ្នាំណា មានសំណាងជាងគេនៅថ្ងៃនេះ? នេះគឺជាចម្លើយ និងអត្ថន័យសម្រាប់អ្នក។
4. Challenge
   English: Can you guess today's luckiest zodiac animal? Three clues: confident, charismatic, and ambitious.
   Khmer: ទាយមើល៖ តើឆ្នាំណា មានសំណាងជាងគេថ្ងៃនេះ? តម្រុយ៖ ជឿជាក់លើខ្លួនឯង ទាក់ទាញចំណាប់អារម្មណ៍ និងមានមហិច្ឆតា។
5. Fact
   English: Today is a Dragon day, and in the twelve-day zodiac cycle, that makes the Dragon today's luckiest sign.
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំរោង ហើយតាមវដ្ដដប់ពីរថ្ងៃ ឆ្នាំរោង គឺជារាសីដែលមានសំណាងជាងគេថ្ងៃនេះ។
★ = used to open this video. Why: Specific and true (only the day's animal has a Great Day), creates curiosity, and every viewer wants to know if it is their sign.

### Audio design
Music: Gentle magical background music with soft chimes and a light Asian flute. Keep it well below the voice (about 20 dB quieter) so every word is clear. Sound effects, used lightly: a soft chime when a lucky fact is revealed, a gentle whoosh on scene changes, a warm swell at the ending. No loud or scary sounds.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)

## 180-second video – YouTube
A 180-second video about the Dragon, today's luckiest animal: 18 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add the reference picture of the Dragon as an ingredient. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice": it copies the style line, the scene and the voice together. Put the clips in order 1 to 18.

### Topic and audience
A three-minute guide to the Dragon's Great Day: meaning, character, love, work, money and lucky details. For people born in the Year of the Dragon and zodiac fans on YouTube Shorts.

### Opening hook – 5 options
★ 1. Daily fortune

### Audio design
Music: Gentle magical background music with soft chimes and a light Asian flute. Keep it well below the voice (about 20 dB quieter) so every word is clear. Sound effects, used lightly: a soft chime when a lucky fact is revealed, a gentle whoosh on scene changes, a warm swell at the ending. No loud or scary sounds.

Today, Wednesday, 21 October 2026, is a Dragon day: a Great Day for everyone born in the Year of the Dragon. Ruler of the clouds and rain, the Dragon symbolizes power, good fortune, and a noble spirit.

Lucky color: gold
Best time: 15:00–17:00
Lucky direction: southwest
Lucky numbers: 1 · 65 · 110
Lucky days of the week: Tuesday and Saturday

Lucky numbers are for fun only, not a prediction or betting advice.

See all 12 signs: https://mybirthsign.com

The pink lotus opens petal by petal in the moonlight, revealing an adorable but mighty golden dragon with big friendly eyes, shimmering scales, flowing whiskers and a glowing pearl on a round golden pedestal. It opens its big sparkling eyes and its golden details begin to glow. Gentle close-up on its face, then pull back.

សម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរោង ថ្ងៃនេះ សំណាងរបស់អ្នកល្អខ្លាំងណាស់។ សូមរក្សាចិត្តឲ្យស្ងប់ ទទួលយកឱកាសល្អៗ ហើយចែករំលែកភាពរីករាយ ដល់មនុស្សជុំវិញខ្លួន។

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
ទិសសំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ គឺទិសនិរតី។ ពេលចាប់ផ្ដើមធ្វើអ្វីថ្មី សូមបែរមុខទៅទិសនិរតី ហើយបោះជំហានទៅមុខ ដោយចិត្តស្ងប់ និងរីករាយ។

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
សម្រាប់លេខសំណាង៖ លេខមួយ លេខហុកសិបប្រាំ និងលេខមួយរយដប់។ សូមសរសេរលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នក នៅក្នុងមតិយោបល់ ហើយតោះនិយាយម្ដងទៀត៖ មួយ ហុកសិបប្រាំ មួយរយដប់!

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)

## Long YouTube video – all 12 signs (about 5 minutes)
A long YouTube video (5:10): today's luckiest animal, an overview, then all 12 signs in order (two 10-second clips each), then a short closing. Lucky numbers are given only for today's lucky signs. Add the 12 character pictures as ingredients (each sign's clips use its own picture). Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Put the clips in order 1 to 31.

### YouTube chapters (paste into the description)
0:00 Introduction
0:40 Rat (ជូត) – Good Day
1:00 Ox (ឆ្លូវ) – Ordinary Day
1:20 Tiger (ខាល) – Ordinary Day
1:40 Rabbit (ថោះ) – Ordinary Day
2:00 Dragon (រោង) – Great Day
2:20 Snake (ម្សាញ់) – Ordinary Day
2:40 Horse (មមី) – Ordinary Day
3:00 Goat (មមែ) – Ordinary Day
3:20 Monkey (វក) – Good Day
3:40 Rooster (រកា) – Ordinary Day
4:00 Dog (ច) – Take It Easy
4:20 Pig (កុរ) – Ordinary Day
4:40 Closing

### Topic and audience
A full daily fortune for all twelve Chinese zodiac signs. For anyone who wants their own sign's rating, lucky color, best time and direction; chapters let each viewer jump to their sign.

### Opening hook – 5 options
★ 1. Curiosity
   English: Three signs are lucky today, and one should take it easy. Where does your sign stand?
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ មានបីឆ្នាំដែលមានសំណាងល្អ និងមួយឆ្នាំដែលគួរសម្រាក។ តើឆ្នាំរបស់អ្នក នៅត្រង់ណា?
2. Question
   English: Which of the twelve zodiac signs is the luckiest today?
   Khmer: តើរាសីណា ក្នុងចំណោមរាសីទាំង 12 មានសំណាងជាងគេថ្ងៃនេះ?
3. Practical
   English: Your lucky color, best time, and direction for today, for all twelve signs, in about five minutes.
   Khmer: ពណ៌ ពេលវេលា និងទិសសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ សម្រាប់រាសីទាំង 12 ក្នុងរយៈពេលប្រហែលប្រាំនាទី។
4. Identity
   English: Whatever your sign, today has a lucky color and a best time just for you.
   Khmer: មិនថាអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំអ្វីទេ ថ្ងៃនេះមានពណ៌សំណាង និងពេលល្អ សម្រាប់អ្នក។
5. Daily fortune
   English: Today is a Dragon day. Here's what that means for every sign.
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំរោង។ មកមើលថា វាមានន័យយ៉ាងណា សម្រាប់រាសីនីមួយៗ។
★ = used to open this video. Why: Every viewer has a sign in this video, so this gives everyone a reason to keep watching until their chapter.

### Audio design
Music: Gentle magical background music with soft chimes and a light Asian flute. Keep it well below the voice (about 20 dB quieter) so every word is clear. Sound effects, used lightly: a soft chime when a lucky fact is revealed, a gentle whoosh on scene changes, a warm swell at the ending. No loud or scary sounds.

## Voice-over – story video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Can one little golden dragon wake the night flowers before the full moon rises? Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple in Cambodia, our story begins.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
But this morning, the flowers fell into a deep sleep. Nothing could wake them. Without their glow, the festival could not begin.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
In the middle of the pond, a golden lotus opened, and out came a little golden dragon. Today was a Dragon day, and the dragon felt something special.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
A baby elephant named Sovann came trotting over. "Little Dragon," he said, "only you can wake the night flowers. Today is your lucky day!"

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Ruler of the clouds and rain, the Dragon symbolizes power, good fortune, and a noble spirit. Sovann smiled. That was exactly why the moon had chosen it.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
To wake the night flowers, the little hero needed three lucky treasures: its lucky color, its lucky path, and its lucky numbers.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
"Let's go!" said the dragon, and it set off on its adventure, with Sovann right by its side.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
People born in the Year of the Dragon are confident, charismatic, and ambitious. Dragons draw attention and often rise to leadership naturally.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
The first treasure was waiting in a garden of a thousand lanterns. Only one shone brightest. It was gold, the dragon's lucky color today.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
The dragon tied a gold ribbon around its neck. "Wear your lucky color," said Sovann, "and good luck stays close to you."

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Next, they came to a crossroads with many paths. One path began to glow. It pointed to the southwest, the dragon's lucky direction.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
But the path climbed a steep stone hill, with a strong wind blowing. The dragon stopped. Sometimes, people born in the Year of the Dragon can come across as proud or impatient with limitations.

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
Sovann came close and smiled. "Breathe slowly," he said. "Trust your heart. You are stronger than you think."

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
Step by step, the dragon climbed the hill. The wind grew softer, and at the top, it laughed with joy.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
The little hero learned that its worries were small, and its good heart was big. Balance and courage always help us grow.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
At last, they reached a great stone door in the temple. Carved above it were bells, stairs, and a riddle about lucky numbers.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
"The door opens only at your best time," said Sovann. For the Dragon today, that is from 3 to 5 in the afternoon. They waited patiently.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
When the time came, the dragon rang the golden bells once, its first lucky number. Ding! The door began to shine.

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
The door opened, and a stairway of light appeared. The dragon climbed 65 glowing steps, its second lucky number, up to the top of the tower.

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
At the top of the tower, they found a single drop of golden dew, sparkling in a crystal shell. It was the magic that wakes the flowers!

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
Around the dew drop, seven little lights twinkled, one for each day of the week. Tuesday and Saturday shone brightest, the Dragon's lucky days.

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
Time was running out. Holding the dew drop carefully, the little hero hurried home, with Sovann right beside it.

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
Back at the pond, everyone was waiting. The night was dark, and the full moon was rising. Was it too late?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
The dragon let the golden dew fall on the flowers. For a moment, nothing happened. Then, one by one, the flowers opened and glowed!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
The night flowers glowed again. Lanterns rose into the sky, and the full-moon festival began. Everyone cheered for the little golden hero!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
The full moon shone down warmly, as if to say thank you. The dragon felt peaceful and proud. It had done its very best.

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
If you were born in the Year of the Dragon, today is your day too. Like our hero, be brave, be kind, and follow your good heart.

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: gold. Best time: from 3 to 5 in the afternoon. Lucky direction: southwest. Lucky numbers: 1, 65, and 110.

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
And so, under the bright full moon, the little golden hero and Sovann fell fast asleep, dreaming of tomorrow.

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
That's your Chinese zodiac fortune for today. See all twelve signs and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign dot com. Follow us for tomorrow's fortune. Have a lucky day!

ពេលដល់ម៉ោង នាគវាយកណ្ដឹងមាស ចំនួនមួយ ដង ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីមួយរបស់វា។ ទីង! ទ្វារចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
ទ្វារបើក ហើយមានជណ្ដើរពន្លឺមួយ លេចឡើង។ នាគឡើងជណ្ដើរភ្លឺ ចំនួនហុកសិបប្រាំ កាំ ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីពីររបស់វា រហូតដល់កំពូលប្រាង្គ។

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
នៅលើកំពូលប្រាង្គ ពួកគេបានរកឃើញដំណក់ទឹកសន្សើមពណ៌មាសមួយ ភ្លឺចែងចាំង ក្នុងសំបកគ្រីស្តាល់។ វាគឺជាវេទមន្ត ដែលដាស់ផ្កា!

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
នៅជុំវិញដំណក់ទឹកសន្សើម មានពន្លឺតូចៗប្រាំពីរ ភ្លឺភ្លឹបភ្លែតៗ មួយសម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនីមួយៗក្នុងសប្ដាហ៍។ ថ្ងៃអង្គារ និងថ្ងៃសៅរ៍ ភ្លឺជាងគេ ដែលជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំរោង។

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
ពេលវេលាជិតអស់ហើយ។ វីរបុរសតូច កាន់ដំណក់ទឹកសន្សើមយ៉ាងប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន ហើយប្រញាប់ត្រឡប់ទៅផ្ទះវិញ ដោយមានសុវណ្ណ នៅក្បែរ។

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
នៅឯស្រះវិញ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា កំពុងរង់ចាំ។ យប់ងងឹត ហើយព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ កំពុងរះ។ តើយឺតពេលហើយឬ?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
នាគទម្លាក់ទឹកសន្សើមមាស ទៅលើផ្កា។ មួយសន្ទុះ គ្មានអ្វីកើតឡើងទេ។ បន្ទាប់មក ផ្កាម្ដងមួយៗ ក៏រីក ហើយភ្លឺចែងចាំង!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
ផ្កាពេលយប់ ភ្លឺឡើងវិញ។ គោមហោះឡើងលើមេឃ ហើយពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ ក៏ចាប់ផ្ដើម។ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា អបអរសាទរវីរបុរសមាសតូច!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។ នាគមានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។ វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរោង ថ្ងៃនេះ ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។ ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌មាស។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងបី រសៀល ដល់ម៉ោងប្រាំ ល្ងាច។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសនិរតី។ លេខសំណាង៖ លេខមួយ លេខហុកសិបប្រាំ និងលេខមួយរយដប់។

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
ហើយដូច្នេះ នៅក្រោមពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ វីរបុរសមាសតូច និងសុវណ្ណ ក៏លង់លក់ ដោយសុបិនអំពីថ្ងៃស្អែក។

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។ អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំងដប់ពីរ និងលេខសំណាង នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។ សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។ សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!

```
បើកតែនៅពេលល្អបំផុតរបស់អ្នកប៉ុណ្ណោះ។

68
00:02:44,462 --> 00:02:45,873
" សម្រាប់នាគថ្ងៃនេះ

69
00:02:45,923 --> 00:02:48,181
គឺម៉ោង 3 រសៀល ដល់ម៉ោង 5 ល្ងាច។

70
00:02:48,231 --> 00:02:49,950
ពួកគេរង់ចាំដោយអត់ធ្មត់។

71
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:52,746
ពេលដល់ម៉ោង នាគវាយកណ្ដឹងមាស

72
00:02:52,796 --> 00:02:56,617
ចំនួន 1 ដង ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីមួយរបស់វា។

73
00:02:56,667 --> 00:02:57,907
ទីង!

74
00:02:57,957 --> 00:02:59,950
ទ្វារចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។

75
00:03:00,000 --> 00:03:03,283
ទ្វារបើក ហើយមានជណ្ដើរពន្លឺមួយ លេចឡើង។

76
00:03:03,333 --> 00:03:05,896
នាគឡើងជណ្ដើរភ្លឺ ចំនួន 65 កាំ

77
00:03:05,946 --> 00:03:09,950
ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីពីររបស់វា រហូតដល់កំពូលប្រាង្គ។

78
00:03:10,000 --> 00:03:11,405
នៅលើកំពូលប្រាង្គ

79
00:03:11,455 --> 00:03:14,677
ពួកគេបានរកឃើញដំណក់ទឹកសន្សើមពណ៌មាសមួយ

80
00:03:14,727 --> 00:03:17,586
ភ្លឺចែងចាំង ក្នុងសំបកគ្រីស្តាល់។

81
00:03:17,636 --> 00:03:19,950
វាគឺជាវេទមន្ត ដែលដាស់ផ្កា!

82
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:21,417
នៅជុំវិញដំណក់ទឹកសន្សើម

83
00:03:21,467 --> 00:03:23,817
មានពន្លឺតូចៗប្រាំពីរ ភ្លឺភ្លឹបភ្លែតៗ

84
00:03:23,867 --> 00:03:26,017
មួយសម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនីមួយៗក្នុងសប្ដាហ៍។

85
00:03:26,067 --> 00:03:28,150
ថ្ងៃអង្គារ និងថ្ងៃសៅរ៍ ភ្លឺជាងគេ

86
00:03:28,200 --> 00:03:29,950
ដែលជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំរោង។

87
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:31,510
ពេលវេលាជិតអស់ហើយ។

88
00:03:31,560 --> 00:03:35,730
វីរបុរសតូច កាន់ដំណក់ទឹកសន្សើមយ៉ាងប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន

89
00:03:35,780 --> 00:03:38,023
ហើយប្រញាប់ត្រឡប់ទៅផ្ទះវិញ

90
00:03:38,073 --> 00:03:39,950
ដោយមានសុវណ្ណ នៅក្បែរ។

91
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:44,485
នៅឯស្រះវិញ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា កំពុងរង់ចាំ។

92
00:03:44,535 --> 00:03:48,438
យប់ងងឹត ហើយព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ កំពុងរះ។

93
00:03:48,488 --> 00:03:49,950
តើយឺតពេលហើយឬ?

94
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:53,027
នាគទម្លាក់ទឹកសន្សើមមាស ទៅលើផ្កា។

95
00:03:53,077 --> 00:03:55,719
មួយសន្ទុះ គ្មានអ្វីកើតឡើងទេ។

96
00:03:55,769 --> 00:03:59,950
បន្ទាប់មក ផ្កាម្ដងមួយៗ ក៏រីក ហើយភ្លឺចែងចាំង!

97
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:01,897
ផ្កាពេលយប់ ភ្លឺឡើងវិញ។

98
00:04:01,947 --> 00:04:03,136
គោមហោះឡើងលើមេឃ

99
00:04:03,186 --> 00:04:06,587
ហើយពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ ក៏ចាប់ផ្ដើម។

100
00:04:06,637 --> 00:04:09,950
អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា អបអរសាទរវីរបុរសមាសតូច!

101
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:13,147
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក

102
00:04:13,197 --> 00:04:15,032
ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។

103
00:04:15,082 --> 00:04:17,819
នាគមានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។

104
00:04:17,869 --> 00:04:19,950
វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

105
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:23,916
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរោង ថ្ងៃនេះ ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។

106
00:04:23,966 --> 00:04:26,588
ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន

107
00:04:26,638 --> 00:04:29,950
មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

108
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,604
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។

109
00:04:31,654 --> 00:04:32,942
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌មាស។

110
00:04:32,992 --> 00:04:35,777
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 3 រសៀល ដល់ម៉ោង 5 ល្ងាច។

111
00:04:35,827 --> 00:04:37,273
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសនិរតី។

112
00:04:37,323 --> 00:04:39,950
លេខសំណាង៖ លេខ 1 លេខ 65 និងលេខ 110។

113
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:44,016
ហើយដូច្នេះ នៅក្រោមពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់

114
00:04:44,066 --> 00:04:46,543
វីរបុរសមាសតូច និងសុវណ្ណ

115
00:04:46,593 --> 00:04:49,950
ក៏លង់លក់ ដោយសុបិនអំពីថ្ងៃស្អែក។

116
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:52,258
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

117
00:04:52,308 --> 00:04:54,373
អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំង 12 និងលេខសំណាង

118
00:04:54,423 --> 00:04:56,232
នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។

119
00:04:56,282 --> 00:04:58,540
សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។

120
00:04:58,590 --> 00:04:59,950
សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!
```

### Subtitles – English (2026-10-21-story.en.srt)
```
1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:02,417
Can one little golden dragon wake the

2
00:00:02,467 --> 00:00:05,150
night flowers before the full moon rises?

3
00:00:05,200 --> 00:00:07,950
Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple

4
00:00:08,000 --> 00:00:09,950
in Cambodia, our story begins.

5
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:11,261
But this morning

6
00:00:11,311 --> 00:00:14,130
the flowers fell into a deep sleep.

7
00:00:14,180 --> 00:00:16,098
Nothing could wake them.

8
00:00:16,148 --> 00:00:17,573
Without their glow

9
00:00:17,623 --> 00:00:19,950
the festival could not begin.

10
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:21,711
In the middle of the pond

11
00:00:21,761 --> 00:00:23,189
a golden lotus opened

12
00:00:23,239 --> 00:00:25,725
and out came a little golden dragon.

13
00:00:25,775 --> 00:00:27,274
Today was a Dragon day

14
00:00:27,324 --> 00:00:29,950
and the dragon felt something special.

15
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:31,529
A baby elephant named

16
00:00:31,579 --> 00:00:33,484
Sovann came trotting over.

17
00:00:33,534 --> 00:00:35,288
"Little Dragon," he said

18
00:00:35,338 --> 00:00:38,070
"only you can wake the night flowers.

19
00:00:38,120 --> 00:00:39,950
Today is your lucky day!"

20
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:41,881
Ruler of the clouds and rain

21
00:00:41,931 --> 00:00:43,743
the Dragon symbolizes power

22
00:00:43,793 --> 00:00:46,019
good fortune, and a noble spirit.

23
00:00:46,069 --> 00:00:46,984
Sovann smiled.

24
00:00:47,034 --> 00:00:48,364
That was exactly why

25
00:00:48,414 --> 00:00:49,950
the moon had chosen it.

26
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:51,983
To wake the night flowers

27
00:00:52,033 --> 00:00:54,259
the little hero needed three

28
00:00:54,309 --> 00:00:56,861
lucky treasures: its lucky color

29
00:00:56,911 --> 00:00:59,950
its lucky path, and its lucky numbers.

30
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:03,018
"Let's go!" said the dragon

31
00:01:03,068 --> 00:01:06,541
and it set off on its adventure

32
00:01:06,591 --> 00:01:09,950
with Sovann right by its side.

33
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:12,593
People born in the Year of the Dragon

34
00:01:12,643 --> 00:01:15,593
are confident, charismatic, and ambitious.

35
00:01:15,643 --> 00:01:18,236
Dragons draw attention and often rise

36
00:01:18,286 --> 00:01:19,950
to leadership naturally.

37
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:22,206
The first treasure was waiting

38
00:01:22,256 --> 00:01:24,837
in a garden of a thousand lanterns.

39
00:01:24,887 --> 00:01:26,717
Only one shone brightest.

40
00:01:26,767 --> 00:01:28,597
It was gold, the dragon's

41
00:01:28,647 --> 00:01:29,950
lucky color today.

42
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:31,847
The dragon tied a gold

43
00:01:31,897 --> 00:01:33,829
ribbon around its neck.

44
00:01:33,879 --> 00:01:36,933
"Wear your lucky color," said Sovann

45
00:01:36,983 --> 00:01:39,950
"and good luck stays close to you."

46
00:01:40,000 --> 00:01:42,410
Next, they came to a crossroads

47
00:01:42,460 --> 00:01:43,680
with many paths.

48
00:01:43,730 --> 00:01:45,506
One path began to glow.

49
00:01:45,556 --> 00:01:47,648
It pointed to the southwest

50
00:01:47,698 --> 00:01:49,950
the dragon's lucky direction.

51
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:52,036
But the path climbed a steep stone hill

52
00:01:52,086 --> 00:01:53,479
with a strong wind blowing.

53
00:01:53,529 --> 00:01:54,495
The dragon stopped.

54
00:01:54,545 --> 00:01:55,672
Sometimes, people born

55
00:01:55,722 --> 00:01:57,864
in the Year of the Dragon can come across

56
00:01:57,914 --> 00:01:59,950
as proud or impatient with limitations.

57
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:02,686
Sovann came close and smiled.

58
00:02:02,736 --> 00:02:05,139
"Breathe slowly," he said.

59
00:02:05,189 --> 00:02:06,837
"Trust your heart.

60
00:02:06,887 --> 00:02:09,950
You are stronger than you think."

61
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:14,236
Step by step, the dragon climbed the hill.

62
00:02:14,286 --> 00:02:16,277
The wind grew softer

63
00:02:16,327 --> 00:02:19,950
and at the top, it laughed with joy.

64
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:23,311
The little hero learned that its worries

65
00:02:23,361 --> 00:02:26,589
were small, and its good heart was big.

66
00:02:26,639 --> 00:02:29,950
Balance and courage always help us grow.

67
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:32,347
At last, they reached a great

68
00:02:32,397 --> 00:02:34,413
stone door in the temple.

69
00:02:34,463 --> 00:02:36,562
Carved above it were bells

70
00:02:36,612 --> 00:02:39,950
stairs, and a riddle about lucky numbers.

71
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:41,477
"The door opens only

72
00:02:41,527 --> 00:02:43,919
at your best time," said Sovann.

73
00:02:43,969 --> 00:02:45,446
For the Dragon today

74
00:02:45,496 --> 00:02:48,271
that is from 3 to 5 in the afternoon.

75
00:02:48,321 --> 00:02:49,950
They waited patiently.

76
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:51,529
When the time came

77
00:02:51,579 --> 00:02:54,775
the dragon rang the golden bells once

78
00:02:54,825 --> 00:02:56,792
its first lucky number.

79
00:02:56,842 --> 00:02:57,845
Ding!

80
00:02:57,895 --> 00:02:59,950
The door began to shine.

81
00:03:00,000 --> 00:03:01,078
The door opened

82
00:03:01,128 --> 00:03:03,559
and a stairway of light appeared.

83
00:03:03,609 --> 00:03:06,191
The dragon climbed 65 glowing steps

84
00:03:06,241 --> 00:03:07,920
its second lucky number

85
00:03:07,970 --> 00:03:09,950
up to the top of the tower.

86
00:03:10,000 --> 00:03:11,719
At the top of the tower

87
00:03:11,769 --> 00:03:14,642
they found a single drop of golden dew

88
00:03:14,692 --> 00:03:16,873
sparkling in a crystal shell.

89
00:03:16,923 --> 00:03:19,950
It was the magic that wakes the flowers!

90
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:22,828
Around the dew drop, seven little lights

91
00:03:22,878 --> 00:03:25,633
twinkled, one for each day of the week.

92
00:03:25,683 --> 00:03:28,223
Tuesday and Saturday shone brightest

93
00:03:28,273 --> 00:03:29,950
the Dragon's lucky days.

94
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:31,913
Time was running out.

95
00:03:31,963 --> 00:03:34,716
Holding the dew drop carefully

96
00:03:34,766 --> 00:03:37,333
the little hero hurried home

97
00:03:37,383 --> 00:03:39,950
with Sovann right beside it.

98
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:43,774
Back at the pond, everyone was waiting.

99
00:03:43,824 --> 00:03:45,538
The night was dark

100
00:03:45,588 --> 00:03:48,381
and the full moon was rising.

101
00:03:48,431 --> 00:03:49,950
Was it too late?

102
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:51,934
The dragon let the golden

103
00:03:51,984 --> 00:03:53,839
dew fall on the flowers.

104
00:03:53,889 --> 00:03:56,299
For a moment, nothing happened.

105
00:03:56,349 --> 00:03:57,569
Then, one by one

106
00:03:57,619 --> 00:03:59,950
the flowers opened and glowed!

107
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:02,281
The night flowers glowed again.

108
00:04:02,331 --> 00:04:04,236
Lanterns rose into the sky

109
00:04:04,286 --> 00:04:06,717
and the full-moon festival began.

110
00:04:06,767 --> 00:04:07,920
Everyone cheered

111
00:04:07,970 --> 00:04:09,950
for the little golden hero!

112
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:12,646
The full moon shone down warmly

113
00:04:12,696 --> 00:04:14,646
as if to say thank you.

114
00:04:14,696 --> 00:04:17,689
The dragon felt peaceful and proud.

115
00:04:17,739 --> 00:04:19,950
It had done its very best.

116
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:22,208
If you were born in the Year

117
00:04:22,258 --> 00:04:25,192
of the Dragon, today is your day too.

118
00:04:25,242 --> 00:04:27,773
Like our hero, be brave, be kind

119
00:04:27,823 --> 00:04:29,950
and follow your good heart.

120
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,908
Let's remember today's luck.

121
00:04:31,958 --> 00:04:33,167
Lucky color: gold.

122
00:04:33,217 --> 00:04:35,964
Best time: from 3 to 5 in the afternoon.

123
00:04:36,014 --> 00:04:37,852
Lucky direction: southwest.

124
00:04:37,902 --> 00:04:39,950
Lucky numbers: 1, 65, and 110.

125
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:43,158
And so, under the bright full moon

126
00:04:43,208 --> 00:04:46,742
the little golden hero and Sovann fell

127
00:04:46,792 --> 00:04:49,950
fast asleep, dreaming of tomorrow.

128
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:51,617
That's your Chinese zodiac

129
00:04:51,667 --> 00:04:52,771
fortune for today.

130
00:04:52,821 --> 00:04:54,053
See all twelve signs

131
00:04:54,103 --> 00:04:56,745
and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign.com.

132
00:04:56,795 --> 00:04:58,860
Follow us for tomorrow's fortune.

133
00:04:58,910 --> 00:04:59,950
Have a lucky day!
```

## Khmer voice – how to get clear Khmer
Every Khmer voice line in this pack is written for speaking: short sentences, a ។ at each pause, and numbers written out in Khmer words (for example ហុកសិបបួន, not 64) so the voice does not read them in English. Subtitles and captions keep the digits.

### If you use Google Flow
Use "Copy + Khmer voice" for each scene; the voice description is already included. Flow's Khmer can be unclear on some clips. Listen to every clip, and remake any clip where words are wrong, cut off or English-sounding. If Khmer stays unclear, make the clips without voice ("Copy") and add a recorded Khmer voice-over instead.

### If you use a text-to-speech app
- Language: Cambodian Khmer (km-KH). Pick a real Khmer voice, for example Microsoft Azure's female voice "km-KH-SreymomNeural" (used by many TTS apps and sites). Do not use an English voice with Khmer text.
- Speed: 0.85× to 0.9×. Pitch: natural. Pause between sentences: normal.
- Paste the Khmer voice-over text from this pack exactly as it is (Khmer script, no romanization).
- Make one scene at a time if the app cuts off long text, then join the clips in your editor.
- Before using a new voice, test it with these two sentences and listen carefully:
  សួស្តីអ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា! សូមស្វាគមន៍មកកាន់ MyBirthSign.com។
  ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំខ្លា។ សូមជឿជាក់លើខ្លួនឯង ហើយកុំភ្លេចអត់ធ្មត់។
  Check that every word is clear Cambodian Khmer, sentence endings are complete, and speed and volume stay even.

### Record it yourself
A native speaker reading the Khmer voice-over slowly and clearly is always the most natural choice.

## Scene 1 – Dragon day
Magical and powerful. An adorable but mighty golden dragon with big friendly eyes, shimmering scales, flowing whiskers and a glowing pearl stands on its glowing lotus pedestal on the pond in front of the Angkor-style temple under a huge full moon. Its eyes light up, a golden aura bursts around it, and it uncoils and soars up into the sky, circling the temple towers with a long ribbon of golden light and swirling clouds. The lotus pond ripples with golden light and lanterns rise into the sky. Dynamic camera: start close on its face, then sweep up and follow it through the air. Cute, majestic and full of energy, matching the reference image.

## Scene 2 – Lucky signs
Magical and powerful. An adorable but mighty golden dragon with big friendly eyes, shimmering scales, flowing whiskers and a glowing pearl, an adorable golden rat with big sparkling eyes, tiny jade-green ear jewels and a proud little smile and a playful, adorable golden monkey with big curious eyes and a cheeky smile fly in from three directions on trails of golden light and land together on glowing lotus pedestals in front of the temple. As they land, a ring of golden energy explodes outward, lotus flowers bloom across the pond and a shower of sparkles and gold coins falls from the sky. They look at each other and cheer. The camera orbits around them in a smooth circle. The animals match the reference images.

## Scene 3 – Dog, take it easy
Premium 3D animated golden style, same characters as the reference pictures. A loyal, adorable golden puppy with big shining eyes and a jeweled collar curls up on a lotus pedestal as a soft protective bubble of golden light forms around it like a shield. Gentle glowing butterflies and petals circle it, and the full moon shines calmly on the still water. The mood is peaceful, safe and magical, not sad. The camera moves in slowly. The dog matches the reference image.

## Scene 4 – Outro
Magical and powerful. Golden light beams shoot up from the temple towers into the night sky and burst into gentle golden fireworks shaped like lotus flowers. Hundreds of floating lanterns rise over the glowing pond and the huge full moon. The camera rises up and pulls back for a wide, epic view, leaving open sky at the top of the frame for a title.

## Scene 5 – Lucky numbers (about 10–12 seconds)
Magical and powerful. A huge glowing golden zodiac wheel rises out of the lotus pond in front of the temple, with twelve cute golden zodiac animals around its rim. It spins faster and faster, shooting beams of golden light and sparkles, then slows to a gentle, shining turn under the full moon. The camera slowly pushes in, keeping open space in the middle and lower part of the frame for the number captions. (Loop the slow-turning part, or slow-zoom a still picture, to fill the time.)

### Description – English
Today is a Dragon day, known traditionally for confidence and fresh energy. Dragon, Rat and Monkey are the luckiest signs, so they may find the day especially encouraging. Dog is asked to take it easy, move at a gentle pace and keep plans simple and calm.

Dragon: gold · 15:00–17:00
Rat: green · 15:00–17:00
Monkey: gold · 07:00–09:00
Dog: red · 11:00–13:00

Today's lucky numbers for the lucky signs (1 to 5 digits):
Dragon  1 · 65 · 110 · 6101 · 61747
Rat  3 · 32 · 299 · 2234 · 33332
Monkey  9 · 97 · 449 · 9999 · 42944

Lucky numbers are for fun only, not a prediction or betting advice.

All 12 signs: https://mybirthsign.com
